Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The macroevolution operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 628. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 259 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_628(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 628. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^259(S, F) is trivial. QED.
